  "fidelity_requirements": "# Fidelity and completion requirements for AC01–AC20\n\nPrepared 10 October 2026 by the independent fidelity-design role. This is an unscored preparation artifact. It specifies evidence required to execute all twenty cards and compare them with earlier methods; it supplies no execution result, case, comparator inventory, regrade or source amendment.\n\n## Authority and interpretation\n\nOperative authority is the full [AC catalog](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/academic-extension/2026-10-10/STRATEGIES.md). The [research dispatch](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/academic-extension/2026-10-10/RESEARCH-DISPATCH.json) is descriptive and unexecuted; its shape-validation pass does not admit a runtime. [Academic foundations](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/academic-extension/2026-10-10/ACADEMIC-FOUNDATIONS.md), [source claim limits](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/academic-extension/2026-10-10/SOURCES.json) and [validation boundaries](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/novelty/academic-extension/2026-10-10/VALIDATION.md) govern interpretation. The [consolidated report](/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/docs/proposals/hammer-bias-strategies/RESEARCH-REPORT.md), especially sections 9, 12, 13.5 and Appendix C, establishes prior failure traps.\n\nAcademic rationale does not validate these complete project adaptations. Human findings do not automatically transfer to agents. Bibliometric association is not causal evidence; measured writing similarity is not an established engineering-agent effect. Novelty is relative to the explicitly inspected repertoire. Actual construction of a reasoning artifact or finite-model replay does not establish deployment, physical feasibility, beneficiary benefit, weight updates or field originality.\n\nFor every row below, preserve actual prompts, supplied source bytes, role outputs and terminal execution witnesses. Record the required function; typed hard constraints, preferences, conventions and unknowns; manipulated variable; information/resources; exact attack, surviving invariant and permissible variable; selected summary or residue; and obligation-retention check. If one investigator performs all passes, report that path rather than claiming independent roles. Preparing a table, prompt, curriculum, route or comparison plan alone never satisfies execution.\n\nThe disposition codes used in each row are:\n\n- **A — applied:** the operative clauses were actually exercised, evidenced in immutable outputs. Application can coexist with infeasibility, no additional novelty or no measured benefit.\n- **N — explicit no-opening:** the procedure actually reached a source-supported terminal boundary preventing its assigned construction. Carry the exact refusal through the selected handoff. Equality with a comparator, by itself, is an applied no-benefit result rather than N.\n- **NA — not-applied:** complete enough evidence shows omission or substitution of an essential operation. Useful substitute work does not repair it.\n- **U — unknown:** essential execution, source, control or comparison evidence is unavailable or invalid. Missing artifacts and gated branches are not zero-effect observations.\n\nA missing comparator can leave application A while the comparison remains U. Assess application, feasibility, repertoire addition, usefulness, burden and learning separately; never collapse these into one success flag.\n\n## Twenty-card execution checklist\n\nEach row requires the common evidence above. T denotes treatment and C its prospective control. The disposition column identifies card-specific evidence in addition to the common definitions.\n\n| Card | Clauses requiring actual execution | Required T/C artifacts and comparison conditions | Disposition evidence and false-success traps |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| **AC01 — Generic parts** | Inventory properties without proposing uses; separately preserve function and prohibited changes; exchange inventories. Remove customary-use labels, generate from an underused property, attack a property use under a supported condition, and build from a surviving property. | T: part/property table, label-free view, source/property/construction chain, attack and retained failure boundary. C: whole-object generation using identical objects, facts, cap and construction resources. Compare against all upstream property uses. | A: actual property-driven construction/search. N: supported property or resource limits leave no admissible alternative. NA: uses only customary action or invents behavior. U: missing property facts or generation witness. A property table or relabeling alone is preparation. |\n| **AC02 — Two analogies** | Inspect two different-surface mechanisms; extract their shared relation before target mapping. Map every essential relation, attack a nontransferable relation and retain at least one source-target mismatch. | T: two source maps, common schema, target map and executable candidate or precise no-transfer finding. C: ordinary analysis with the same two descriptions and matched resources. Single-source comparison is separate and conditional on information control. | A: evidenced schema induction and target construction. N: cited target entity, relation or resource is absent. NA: metaphor replaces relational mapping or only one source is used. U: source maps or target evidence missing. Naming two analogies alone is preparation. |\n| **AC03 — One grammar rule** | Enumerate admissibility rules; distinguish requirements from conventions using evidence. Change exactly one confirmed nonessential rule, attack that classification and construct an operation excluded by the original grammar while preserving outcome. | T: old/new grammar, one-rule delta, newly expressible operation and preservation argument. C: original-grammar search, including equal-budget broader original-grammar search. Retain unchanged hard constraints. | A: one-rule transformation actually used. N: no confirmed mutable convention or no admissible operation after the search. NA: candidate already expressible or essential outcome weakened. U: rule status unsupported. A proposed grammar change without subsequent search is preparation. |\n| **AC04 — Arrangement first** | Use a closed component set. Save bounded valid arrangements before target interpretation, assign functions, swap assembly/interpretation roles once, attack an interpretation and reconstruct a surviving arrangement. | T: ordered arrangement → interpretation → task-fit ledger, rebuildable details and rejected interpretations. C: function-first design with identical components, candidate count and resources. Preserve operation-level differences. | A: ordering and swap evidenced, followed by interpretation/reconstruction. N: permissible arrangements have no supported task-fit interpretation. NA: function labels preceded assembly or interpretation introduced components/properties. U: ordering or inventory unavailable. An arrangement list alone is preparation. |\n| **AC05 — Translation** | Translate identical facts to another representation; reconstruct the verbal problem and check loss/equivalence. Attack inconsistency, then construct from a newly visible relation without adding a fact. | T: both representations, round-trip obligation check, relation and changed operation. C: ordinary analysis plus an additional-format control to distinguish representation from extra text/information; match source facts and resources. | A: verified translation actually informs construction. N: no usable new relation survives the checks. NA: new facts, dropped constraints or simple redraw presented as operation. U: equivalence or construction evidence missing. Diagram preparation alone does not execute the card. |\n| **AC06 — Failure-driven frame** | Actually run a bounded prototype/replay or explicitly modeled failure; link its failure to one framing assumption. Try one justified revised frame and produce a new candidate, preserving the required outcome and recording every scope delta. | T: frame → candidate → failure evidence → justified revision → new candidate, with modeled/observed labels. C: fixed-frame iteration under the same trial budget and evidence. Owner facts may justify a revision; convenient invented needs may not. | A: evidence-bound revision tried. N: failure exposes no permissible frame change. NA: revision lacks a source reason or weakens success criteria. U: failure cannot be reproduced/interpreted. A predicted failure or reframe plan without the stated chain is preparation. |\n| **AC07 — Breadth/depth** | Spend prospectively separate category-search and development budgets. Cover causally distinct categories without polishing, develop a predeclared subset, attack category duplication and resource gaps, retain one closed option and unresolved alternatives where available. | T: mechanism-category map, developed construction, unresolved ledger and actual spent budget. C: breadth-only, depth-only and fixed mixed allocation at equal total resources. Do not choose the allocation after observing winners. | A: allocated searches and development actually performed. N: source-supported search yields no admissible category/construction. NA: renamed families count as breadth or unfinished sketches as development. U: spent resources unknown. Equal no-benefit mixed performance still counts A. Budget declaration alone is preparation. |\n| **AC08 — Agent context branch** | Declare the agent branch separately. Compare actual continued history with an actual fresh context receiving a controlled essential fact pack; preserve prerequisites and tensions before resumption and audit extra cues/resources. | T/C: context manifests, literal fact packs and retained history, preparation/resumption outputs, matched budgets and new feasible mechanisms. Human interruption versus uninterrupted work belongs to a separate human protocol and remains unrun here. | A: context/history manipulation and resumed search witnessed. N: no supported opening survives resumption. NA: waiting is labeled incubation or history is not changed. U: context exposure cannot be established. A pack or delay alone is preparation; agent results cannot substantiate human incubation. |\n| **AC09 — Example timing** | Save task-fact-only generation before example exposure. Separately record example features; then actually expose the same examples, revise, and attack useful transfer versus copying. Preserve independent candidates alongside revisions. | T: before/after locked sets, exposure order and example-feature/mechanism provenance. C: examples-before generation and matched fact-only generation; match facts, examples, resource totals and caps except assigned timing. | A: real before/exposure/after sequence. N: no feasible candidate survives the source conditions. NA: examples leaked before the saved first set or copying is called addition. U: exposure order/sets missing. No gain or lost useful knowledge is an applied negative result. A quarantine instruction alone is preparation. |\n| **AC10 — One connection** | Document closure of a verified core. Change exactly one supported interface/relation/dependency, localize uncertainty, construct its interface contract, preserve core obligations and produce a separating test. | T: core closure, stable boundary, changed-link delta, contract and localized test. C: whole-system replacement and ordinary improvement of the unchanged core under declared resources. Inspect upstream link operations. | A: assigned connection actually changed. N: required link is immutable or no insertion meets obligations. NA: another connection or coordination task substitutes for the assigned link; hidden multiple changes. U: core closure or interface facts absent. A link proposal alone is preparation. |\n| **AC11 — Soft-constraint ladder** | Confirm one soft constraint from sources, fix ordered settings, construct at declared settings and actually try an adjacent setting after attacking the activation cause. Keep all hard requirements and the outcome unchanged. | T/C: setting → mechanisms → usefulness/cost map, ordinary setting, adjacent comparison, threshold and burden/source status. Equal search budget per setting; freeze the ladder before outcomes. | A: ladder construction/comparison performed. N: no source-confirmed soft constraint or no admissible operation across settings. NA: multiple variables relaxed or a hard constraint changed. U: constraint classification/setting evidence missing. No informative change is an applied defeat; a ladder design alone is preparation. |\n| **AC12 — Constructive opening** | Defend a candidate and attack a source-supported dependency. Save one identical criticism for both conditions. Actually compare fresh builders receiving critique alone versus critique plus explicit invariant/permitted-variable contract; construct from that variable or carry terminal refusal. | T/C: failure → invariant → variable → construction → defeat chain, literal attack equality, fresh-builder information manifests, repertoire contraction/expansion and upstream builder comparison. Match resources and attack evidence. | A: opening-to-construction obligation exercised. N: no supported permissible variable. NA: repairs the incumbent only as purported expansion, substitutes a problem or invents an opening. U: shared attack or builder inputs missing. Merely appending an opening contract without builder use is preparation. |\n| **AC13 — Countermodel** | Type the premise as implication, equivalence, necessity or generalization. Search for function-without-tool, attack logic and admissibility separately, construct under the missing-tool condition, and state a separating test. | T: typed claim, admissible countermodel/construction or no-opening proof, feasibility gaps and test. C: ordinary reframing with equal facts/resources. Keep required function fixed; inspect any upstream equivalent. | A: typed challenge and constrained search executed. N: source conditions defeat every permitted missing-tool construction examined. NA: inverse/converse treated as deduction, changed function or invented resource. U: claim/feasibility evidence absent. A reversed slogan or logic tutorial alone is preparation. |\n| **AC14 — Pool then dissent** | Generate by genuinely nonoverlapping concerns under common essential facts. Save and join all initial proposals before dialogue; exchange a contradictory dependency/prediction, then construct from that tension. Preserve disagreement and initial/post-exchange provenance. | T: actual independent pool, joined inputs, tension ledger and post-exchange construction. C: immediate dialogue with equal total work and common facts. Trace additions against the full initial pool, not just final summaries. | A: independence/order/exchange chain witnessed. N: valid tension has no permissible construction. NA: early dialogue contaminates independence, only consensus language appears or an initial mechanism is relabeled addition. U: one role/order/input absent. Concern labels and dialogue plans alone are preparation. |\n| **AC15 — Fixed-pool frontier** | Freeze one pool; separately rate originality, fit, burden and uncertainty. Attack lineage/utility claims. Apply a predeclared frontier rule and ordinary selection to the identical pool; retain both selected sets and displaced options. | T/C: pool digest, ratings with reasons, selection-rule version, selected sets and displaced-option ledger. Generation stays fixed; originality includes upstream lineage. Report tradeoffs and qualified useful unfamiliar options rather than novelty score alone. | A: both selection procedures run. N: source/rating evidence leaves no admissible useful unfamiliar selection opening. NA: regenerates/enlarges the pool, changes rule after outcomes or sacrifices a required function. U: pool/ratings/ordinary choice missing. Better selection cannot be credited as generation. A frontier formula alone is preparation. |\n| **AC16 — Agent training/transfer** | Teach one named operation through varied practice with matched active control. Prepare holdouts before training outcomes. Declare external retained state; actually test baseline, immediate assistance, retained retrieval and unfamiliar tasks with both retrieval-assisted and unassisted agent conditions. Attack answer leakage and surface repetition. | Curriculum/control, untouched retention/near/far sets, state snapshots, retrieval rules, method card with conditions/counterexamples excluding holdout answers, and actual fresh test outputs. Match examples, practice, feedback and resources. Declare near/far distinctions prospectively. | A: training and declared agent test cells executed. N: appropriate trained refusal on an inadmissible holdout, not substitute for other tests. NA: repeats taught answer or hides retained state. U: missing holdouts/test stages. Gains vanishing without scaffolding are negative evidence. Curriculum alone is preparation; human delayed unassisted outcomes remain unrun. |\n| **AC17 — Retained revision** | Classify an actual evidenced failure; revise exactly one retrieval cue, applicability condition or construction obligation in an actual retained card. Preserve invariant/counterexample, attack overfitting, then use old versus revised cards on fresh, separately frozen held-out cases with matched feedback exposure. | Failure taxonomy/evidence, immutable old/new versions, one-variable diff, exact retention/retrieval path, heldout isolation and actual later comparison outputs. A later comparison plan is insufficient under the objective to run the card. | A: revision saved and fresh held-out use witnessed. N: evidenced failure offers no legitimate method change or supports an explicit applicability refusal. NA: several variables/solved answer inserted, same-case reuse or failure reclassification. U: actual revision/use absent. Reflection and proposed edits alone are preparation; no persistent improvement without results. |\n| **AC18 — Actual beneficiary** | Include actual beneficiary evidence in every condition. Trace producer resources and beneficiary work, attack hidden burden transfer, change an operation while preserving the valued function, and retain added upkeep/uncertainty plus an observable consequence. | T/C: same cited beneficiary facts, burden path, changed candidate and consequence; ordinary optimization versus explicit perspective-taking. A direct user's task preference can qualify if quoted verbatim with speaker, message/record provenance and relevance. Separate forecasts from measured burden. | A: actual evidence grounds the operation. N: sourced burdens/constraints admit no supported change. NA: simulated perspective/invented burden substitutes for actual evidence or shifts work to an unexamined person. U: beneficiary provenance unavailable. A persona or burden ledger alone is preparation; agent estimates do not demonstrate actual relief. |\n| **AC19 — Reason separation** | Freeze pool/evidence/stakes. Actually evaluate feasibility and inspect decision reasons separately; require cited exclusions distinguishing infeasibility, missing evidence, burden, outcome mismatch and unfamiliarity. Offer a bounded discriminating-test decision for unresolved options, retaining valid technical rejections. | T/C: candidate → reason → evidence → next-test ledger and actual ordinary/reason-separated selections on identical alternatives/facts. Record confidence and justified choice separately; executing a chosen real-world test requires its own evidence. | A: reason separation changes or substantiates an actual decision. N: all exclusions are source-supported and no permissible unresolved-test opening remains. NA: necessary rejection masked or uncertainty converted to benefit/failure. U: reasons/evidence/choice absent. A rejection taxonomy alone is preparation. |\n| **AC20 — Instantiated source routes** | Save pre-assistance candidates. Actually supply shared-seed and distinct verified source-route conditions, keeping essential facts common. Track examples/relations/facts seeding each candidate, map causal families, attack lexical diversity and retain rare feasible mechanisms with dependencies. | T/C: literal routed packs, route/source IDs and digests, exposure logs, coverage/overlap/cost record, source/candidate graph, mechanism-family/usefulness table. Match resources; disclose unavoidable information differences. Compare pool quality and cross-output similarity separately. | A: distinct routes are instantiated and joined, with actual provenance. N: verified routes yield no permissible feasible addition. NA: role/persona labels or words substitute for changed sources, or infeasible variety is counted as useful. U: routed bytes/exposure/provenance missing. A routing plan alone is preparation; more information cannot silently count as routing benefit. |\n\n## Prospective protocol and adapter gates\n\nBefore scored launch, replace the descriptive dispatch with a bounded execution contract while preserving its original file. Name execution owner, all twenty selected card versions, exact case/source hashes, condition units, prompts, role bindings, information boundaries, selected handoff branch, waves, write scopes and missing-data policy. Existing user authorization covers the requested execution; runtime admission and evidence gates still require actual artifacts, not another permission ritual.\n\n1. **Context frozen:** require available method-specific controls, typed constraints, outcome and defeat criteria, ordinary/component/upstream comparison scope, budget and unit of analysis. Freeze training/holdout separation and AC17 feedback/revision timing. Obtain actual AC18 beneficiary evidence and AC20 route packs before admitting those cells. Unknown constraint status never permits relaxation.\n2. **Adapter preflight:** use realistic unscored fixtures for numbered fact references, membership, branch selection and lifecycle failures. Literal source-reference grammar in prompts and validator must agree. Check missing output, invalid references, terminal refusal, malformed handoff and budget exhaustion paths. Pure fixture/replay success is no native strategy execution.\n3. **Information and order:** enforce before/after saves for AC04/09/14/20, fresh boundaries for AC08/12/16/17 and fixed pools for AC15/19. Bind every handoff to saved bytes. Count any reused control only when its information, frame, outcome, resources and condition truly match; disclose correlated comparisons.\n4. **Handoff faithful:** verify that the selected branch carries the exact assigned operation, invariants, resources, rival predictions, uncertainty and terminal refusal. Checking mechanical projection alone is insufficient. A summary dropping refusal while residue retains it fails this gate, as prior R07 did. No substituted connection or antecedent observation can satisfy the assigned move.\n5. **Comparisons complete:** obtain independent application and outcome/repertoire judgments with preserved raw disagreements. Audit empty controls as actual source-backed refusals. Compare final cards against proposals, attacks, summaries, residue and intermediate builders to detect upstream duplication. Separate unavailable contrast, applied defeat and no-opening.\n6. **Closeout/claim ceiling:** all actual roles joined and closed; files stable and bound to receipts. Reconcile scheduled dispositions, actual starts, FINALs, invalid outputs and gates. Leave earlier raw grades and failed comparisons unchanged. A separately declared fresh coverage study may repair coverage, never the original score.\n\n## The 260-call ceiling and audit obligations\n\nThe ceiling is overall, including preparation, unscored native preflight, scored conditions, reviewers, auditors, report roles, failed starts and any additional native turns. The execution owner must freeze a ledger satisfying:\n\n`actual calls already spent + remaining admitted calls + reserved required closure/audit calls <= 260`.\n\nA gate without an invocation and pure deterministic tooling are dispositions/tool operations, not native calls. Conversely, failed or invalid native outputs consume calls. Do not multiply twenty by thirteen and assume overhead disappears. Reserve completion and report audit capacity before launching generation; admit new work only if its required downstream reviews fit the remaining budget. Optional rounds stop at repeated structure/no new evidence; three rounds is a maximum, not a completion substitute.\n\nBudget compression may reuse genuinely matched baselines, batch independent reviews with explicit per-card judgments, or limit claim scope to a coverage pilot. It cannot drop essential comparisons, replace training by a curriculum, replace held-out use by a plan, invent beneficiary evidence or describe planned source diversity as delivered. If essential cells do not fit, retain their U/gated status and state that the all-twenty objective remains incomplete.\n\nThe audit must bind actual agent/role IDs, launch and join observations, terminal witnesses, literal prompts, first-observed locked output hashes and validation results; preserve create-exclusive save evidence and any timing departures. File mtimes are write observations, not exact spawn timestamps. Root-observed evidence does not certify unobserved pre-FINAL access or independently authenticate platform events. Report actual calls, word/token/time data when available, and missing cost data; word caps do not prove equal compute.\n\n## Essential clauses agent evidence cannot establish by itself\n\nHuman incubation (AC08), human delayed unassisted retention (AC16), human motivation and measured beneficiary relief require corresponding human/world observations. They must remain unrun or unknown when only agent branches run. A finite replay can substantiate AC06's explicitly modeled failure; it cannot validate its physical rules. Construct a provisional arrangement before interpreting it\n\n**Bottleneck:** A fixed interpretation makes every combination an elaboration of the incumbent.\n\n**Operative move:** Generate a small set of valid arrangements of known components before assigning each a target role; then explore their possible functions.\n\n**Procedure and handoff:** Supply the same closed component set to both lanes. Lane A assembles bounded arrangements; Lane B assigns interpretations subject to the invariant. Swap roles once. Attack one interpretation and have a builder reconstruct a surviving arrangement. The ordering intervention is new preparation for this program; the cognitive idea is established.\n\n",
